### 2026-07-29 12:16:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Holds Near Multi-Week Highs — Corn futures held above $4.5 per bushel, staying near their highest level since late May as geopolitical disruptions raised concerns over global supplies, while persistent dry weather in parts of the US Midwest threatened yields. The USDA said that 63% of the country's corn crop was rated good-to-excellent, down from 67% a week earlier and below market expectations. Meanwhile, continued attacks between Russia and Ukraine raised concerns over Black Sea grain exports, with damage to port infrastructure and shipping routes threatening supplies from one of the world's key exporting regions. Additionally, renewed fighting in the Middle East constrained fertilizer shipments through the Strait of Hormuz and pushed crude oil prices higher. Higher energy prices supported corn by improving the outlook for ethanol demand. The market also remained underpinned by the USDA's latest WASDE report, which cut 2026/27 US ending stocks more than expected while raising export forecasts.

【Bank of England probes London banks' rising Asian equity exposure, warns on concentrated AI bets】The Bank of England is investigating rapid growth in London-based investment banks' exposure to Asian equities; officials say they want to avoid highly concentrated bets on a handful of AI-related names. Hedge funds and other institutions have been channeling capital into companies supplying AI semiconductor infrastructure in Asia, including SK Hynix and TSMC. The central bank says these Asian stocks are becoming an increasingly significant part of major UK brokers' books and appear concentrated in a small set of AI-linked firms. Clients may be taking additional risk through high‑leverage option positions in Asian markets, and some institutions may be raising capital from Asian retail investors to fund trading — a potential liquidity vulnerability if those retail holders sell quickly in a stress event.

Soybean Futures Near 2-Week Low — Soybean futures fell below $12.1 per bushel, approaching a two-week low as expectations of ample global supplies offset support from strong US export demand and deteriorating crop conditions. The USDA lowered its good-to-excellent rating for the nation's soybean crop to 63% from 66% a week earlier, reflecting persistent heat in parts of the Midwest. At the same time, China's Sinograin plans to auction 500,000 metric tons of imported soybeans, its largest sale since January, to free up storage ahead of new US arrivals, reinforcing expectations for continued US export demand. The USDA also reported multiple soybean export sales to China earlier this month, including 340,000 metric tons and 264,000 metric tons, alongside 256,634 metric tons to Mexico. Meanwhile, Brazil is expected to export a record 115.4 million metric tons of soybeans in 2026, according to crushers group Abiove, raising its outlook by 1.1% from a June projection while increasing soy crushing estimates.

【感染致死率上升 智利釋出漢坦病毒公共衛生警報】智利衛生部28日釋出公報說，由於智利今年以來漢坦病毒感染致死率有所上升，政府決定針對該病毒釋出公共衛生警報。警報覆蓋智利16個大區中的13個，將持續至2027年7月31日。 據公報介紹，漢坦病毒在智利呈地方性流行，通常由齧齒動物傳播，衛生部一直在監測其動態。截至上週，智利今年以來共記錄到46例漢坦病毒感染病例，其中18例死亡，感染致死率高達39%；而2025年這一比例為18%。 公報說，此次警報屬於預防性措施，可在出現嚴重健康風險的緊急情況時賦予相關部門特殊權力，以加強監測、及時調配資源、聘用專業人員、開展衛生防控行動，並透過直接採購方式快速獲取物資。公報同時強調，該警報並不意味著疫情暴發或醫療系統已不堪重負。 智利衛生部專家呼籲民眾保持警惕，包括避免接觸齧齒動物、注意房屋通風、妥善存放食物等，一旦出現發熱、劇烈肌肉疼痛、呼吸困難等症狀且曾到過鼠類常出沒地區，應立即前往醫療機構，並主動報告相關情況。(新華社)

### 2026-07-29 11:45:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Singapore #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Singapore Producer Inflation Slows in June — Singapore’s domestic supply price index climbed 30.3% year-on-year in June 2026, slowing from a downwardly revised 34.1% jump in the previous month. The slowdown is largely driven by moderating costs for mineral fuels, lubricants and related materials (46.9% vs 76.7% in May), crude materials (13.0% vs 13.3%), and chemical and chemical products (13.4% vs 21.5%). Meanwhile, price growth increased for machinery and transport equipment (35.1% vs 27.5%) and manufactured goods (5.7% vs 5.4%). On the other hand, prices declined for food and live animals, down 1.9% after a 2.5% drop in the prior month. On a monthly basis, the domestic supply price index fell 2.5% in June, marking its second monthly decline of the year and a steeper decrease than the 2.0% drop recorded in the last month.

【BNP Paribas: Fed statement likely to signal willingness to act if necessary】BNP Paribas Markets 360 expects the Fed to hold rates, while not ruling out a surprise hike. Its baseline is one hike in December, but it sees a substantial risk the FOMC will harden inflation wording in the statement, effectively putting a September rate move back on the table. Wording on price stability will be the focal point; the statement is likely to indicate a readiness to act if needed. BNP Paribas warns absence of such language would not preclude a September hike, nor would inclusion guarantee one. At the press conference, the Fed speaker is expected to follow June’s pattern — brief opening remarks, concise answers and very limited forward guidance — and, if the statement is largely unchanged from June, opening remarks should mirror his congressional testimony on inflation, labour-market data, the outlook and the commitment to restore price stability.

### 2026-07-29 11:31:27  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palm Oil Rebounds on Firmer Edible Oils, Export Optimism — Malaysian palm oil futures bounced back to trade above MYR 4,650 per tonne, recovering from recent losses as stronger Dalian palm oil and Chicago soybean oil prices improved sentiment. Higher crude oil prices, supported by a drawdown in U.S. crude inventories, also boosted the biodiesel outlook and lent additional support. On the demand front, cargo surveyors estimated Malaysia's palm oil exports for July 1–25 increased between 8.1% and 15.9% from the same period in June. Meanwhile, palm oil imports by top buyer India are expected to rise between July and October as tighter edible oil supplies ahead of the festive season lift buying interest. Meanwhile, Indonesia is reportedly seeking a U.S. exemption for palm oil under the new 10% tariff regime to safeguard its export competitiveness. However, gains were tempered by a stronger ringgit and reports that European Union palm oil imports for the 2026/27 marketing year, which began in July, plunged 39% yoy to 0.13 million tonnes.

【國家統計局：新動能日益成為經濟穩定執行的關鍵支撐】國家統計局國民經濟綜合統計司副司長王冠華在29日推出的“中國經濟圓桌會”大型全媒體訪談節目中說，今年上半年，以高階製造、數智經濟、現代服務為代表的新動能對經濟增長貢獻率超過四成，日益成為我國經濟在複雜環境中保持穩定執行的關鍵支撐。“新舊動能加快轉換已經成為當前經濟執行最鮮明的特徵之一。這種轉變不是少數行業的零星突破，而是普遍發生在各行各業的提質升級中。”王冠華說。王冠華表示，新產業、新業態等快速發展不是短期偶然迸發的紅利，而是多年來我國加強關鍵核心技術攻關、推動科技創新和產業創新深度融合、深耕產業轉型和培育創新主體的結果，是穩紮穩打、厚積薄發的過程。“經過多年佈局和發展，我國新興產業成長壯大、積厚成勢，人工智慧成為新舊動能轉換的加速器，賦能千行百業、走進千家萬戶。”王冠華說。王冠華表示，除了新興產業，煉化、化纖等傳統行業也透過開展智慧化改造、更新新工藝、佈局新產品，煥發新的生機和活力。中國經濟增長正全面轉向依靠創新驅動的高質量發展軌道。（新華社）

Singapore Stocks Hit Fresh Record High — Singapore's stocks rose 23 points, or 0.4%, to a record high of 5,640 in Wednesday morning trade, after being flat in the previous session, hitting a fresh record high, mainly boosted by gains in the communication services, technology, and financial sectors. Strong domestic economic data, political stability, and the attractiveness of dividend-paying stocks buoyed the index, with gains in banking stocks offsetting the negative sentiment from the global tech selloff. Traders shrugged off the MAS's tightening of monetary policy on Monday, while hoping the meeting between US President Trump and Israeli Prime Minister Netanyahu could ease tensions in the Middle East amid rising oil prices. However, traders remained cautious ahead of the Fed's monetary policy decision, China's PMI data, and Singapore's Q2 unemployment rate. Among the top gainers were Hongkong Land Holdings (3.7%), Jardine Matheson (2.0%), Keppel (1.5%), City Developments (1.3%), OCBC (1.1%), UOB (0.8%), and DBS (0.5%).

【小摩：中國電池供應鏈8月產量料環比加速增長，寧德時代仍為首選】摩根大通發表報告指，ZE Consulting最新預測顯示，中國電池供應鏈環比動能仍將持續強勁，前六大電池廠商8月計劃產量環比增長8%、同比增長65%，符合8月典型季節性規律。7-8月平均產量亦預計較次季月均水平高出13%，可比或強於往年同期的環比增幅。最新排產計劃顯示，在進入季節性更強的8–9月生產旺季之際，電池需求基本面依然健康。寧德時代繼續表現突出，次季產量同比約增80%，7月及8月均預計同比增長逾70%，領先多數同業約40-60%的增幅。寧德時代仍是該行在中國電池供應鏈中的首選。在二線企業中，該行指中創新航年內產量增長最高，同比增逾90%，追蹤高於其55%的銷量增長目標。

### 2026-07-29 11:01:36  #公司

【雷軍回應小米澎程為什麼要做增程】雷軍微博發文：最近我被問得最多的兩個問題：小米澎程為什麼要做增程？現在進入，是不是太晚？ 先說第一個。增程還是純電，從來不是路線之爭，而是產品定義和體驗追求的選擇。 SU7和YU7系列，定位“駕駛者之車”，追求極致的動力響應和操控體驗，純電是最簡潔、最純粹的答案。 而澎程系列，定位智慧可變大空間SUV，要真正承載生活的各種可能性。既要跑得遠，也能用得久，補能要自由，這時候，增程便是那把鑰匙，讓乘坐空間與續航里程同時釋放，每次出發都自在從容。同時，大型SUV的方盒子造型，風阻本就不小，加上全尺寸車身的自重，如果要做成純電車，就必須要有超大電池，電池又進一步加大車身重量，反過來影響續航。這是物理上的限制，而增程，恰好把它解開了。 那現在才出增程車，晚了嗎？ 我的回答是，不晚。 和坊間不少觀點不同，增程市場遠未飽和，反而大有可為。

### 2026-07-29 11:00:58  #宏觀 #兩會

【國家智慧財產權局：我國商標領域立法從“區域性修改”向“系統完善”轉變升級】國務院新聞辦公室舉行“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會，國家智慧財產權局介紹，今年6月26日，十四屆全國人大常委會第二十三次會議審議透過新修訂的商標法，這是商標法自1983年施行以來的首次全面修訂，體現了我國商標領域立法從“區域性修改”向“系統完善”的轉變升級。本次修改亮點：一是突出誠實守信。明確強調申請註冊和使用商標，應當遵循誠信原則，不得濫用權利損害國家利益、社會公共利益或者他人的合法權益。二是完善商標註冊條件。進一步拓寬商標可註冊要素類別，規定動態標誌及其與相關要素的組合，例如手機開機動畫可以作為商標申請註冊，回應新技術、新業態企業需求。三是健全馳名商標制度。一方面，將商標馳名情況作出“認定”的表述修改為“確認”，遏制過度追求馳名認定的現象。另一方面，不再以註冊與否區分馳名商標能否跨類保護，更好對接高標準國際經貿規則。四是加大商標管理力度。完善閒置商標退出機制，推動解決商標“注而不用”問題。嚴厲打擊以誤導公眾的方式使用註冊商標的行為。五是從嚴監管商標代理。明確了從業準則、受託事項、備案義務等，細化了違法情形及處罰標準，進一步加強行業自律，促進商標代理行業健康發展。六是明確部門職責。進一步明確商標註冊、管理、執法等部門的職責，完善商標案件行刑銜接機制和跨部門協作機制。明確要求國務院商標管理部門加強資訊化、智慧化商標公共服務體系建設，提升商標業務辦理的便利化程度。修改後的商標法將於明年1月1日正式施行。

Wheat Futures Climb Over 1% — Wheat futures rose more than 1% to around $6.70 per bushel in late July, recovering further from sharp losses on July 27 as escalating risks to Black Sea grain shipments stoked concerns over tightening global supplies. Concerns over Black Sea grain exports worsened after three major Russian grain terminals reportedly limited truck deliveries, citing heightened security risks and logistical disruptions linked to recent attacks in the Sea of Azov. Further raising fears of supply disruptions, a missile strike hit the Russian port city of Taganrog, a key grain-export hub on the Sea of Azov. The latest developments came as Russia and Ukraine continued targeting vessels and port facilities, raising concerns about the security of one of the world's most critical grain-export corridors and heightening fears of disruptions to global shipments. Meanwhile, investors watched reports that Ukraine was seeking to maintain vessel traffic through its major export ports, despite official denials.

【國家智慧財產權局：完善新興領域智慧財產權保護制度】國家智慧財產權局在國務院新聞辦公室舉行的釋出會上介紹，當前，人工智慧、量子科技、新一代資訊科技、生物醫藥等新興產業和未來產業，技術迭代快、業態跨界深，對建立健全智慧財產權保護制度提出了新的要求。國家“十五五”規劃《綱要》明確提出要“完善新興領域智慧財產權保護制度”。近年來，開展的一些重點工作也已取得一定成效。一是新興領域智慧財產權保護制度不斷創新。持續推進審查模式創新，不斷提升智慧財產權審查質量和效率，有效適應新興領域發展迅猛、創新高效、迭代快速的特點。截至今年6月底，我國國內（不含港澳臺）戰略性新興產業發明專利有效量已達174.6萬件，較上年底增長了4.3%，在多個產業領域佈局了一批關鍵核心技術專利。二是新興領域智慧財產權保護成果加速轉化。圍繞物聯網、人工智慧等新興領域，高標準建設產業智慧財產權運營中心，推進新興領域專利產業化。目前已批覆的65家智慧財產權運營中心中有48家涉及積體電路、生物醫藥、航空航天、6G等新興產業和未來產業，佔比超過70%。持續開展資料智慧財產權保護實踐探索，相關登記證書已在司法裁判、資產入表等場景中成功應用，累計實現融資增信金額近160億元，交易許可和證券化金額超20億元。三是新興領域智慧財產權保護環境持續最佳化。印發《關於進一步加強光伏產業智慧財產權保護工作的意見》《“網際網路+”智慧財產權保護工作方案》等政策檔案，提升重點領域智慧財產權保護效能。國家智慧財產權局表示，新興領域範疇廣泛、業態多元。下一步，將分別聚焦新興領域不同產業的發展實踐，從智慧財產權端為新興領域發展提供效果更優的授權確權模式，界定更清晰的權利歸屬、保護範圍和保護形式，執行更順暢的協同保護體系和侵權應對機制。

### 2026-07-29 10:50:57  #MKT News

【Foreign worker numbers rise; South Korea population grows for third straight year amid super-aged status】Statistics Korea said South Korea’s population rose for a third consecutive year to 51.8 million in 2025, up about 12,000 from 2024, as an increase in foreign residents offset a continued decline in Korean nationals. Korean citizens fell for a fifth straight year to 49.7 million while foreign residents climbed to a record 2.10 million, or 4.1% of the population. South Korea formally joins the ranks of ‘super-aged’ societies, with those aged 65+ at 20.7% of the population; the 15–64 age group slipped below 70% for the first time. The working-age population fell by 393,000 year-on-year. Foreign residents are increasingly concentrated in the labor force: 89.3% are of working age versus 68.4% of Korean nationals; foreign resident numbers have risen since 2022 while Korean nationals have declined annually since 2021.

Palladium Extends Losses — Palladium futures fell to around $1,260 per ounce, remaining under pressure as the dollar hovered near a one-month high and investors awaited the Federal Reserve's policy decision. A stronger dollar made greenback-priced metals more expensive for overseas buyers, weighing on palladium. Meanwhile, Middle East tensions offered little support as markets monitored developments in the Strait of Hormuz after Iran launched missiles at US forces and Gulf states proposed a framework to manage the key shipping route. Over the past month, palladium has gained 4.75% and was up 3.26% from a year earlier.

### 2026-07-29 10:38:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

AUS 10Y Yield Retreats from Multi-Week Highs — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 4.9%, easing from multi-week highs as softer inflation figures reduced expectations of further interest rate hikes. Headline inflation unexpectedly eased to a four-month low of 3.8% in June from both May's reading and forecasts of 4.0%, while monthly consumer prices unexpectedly fell 0.1% for a second straight month. Meanwhile, the closely watched trimmed mean inflation rate rose 3.6% annually, below expectations of 3.7%, while quarterly core inflation increased 0.8%, also undershooting forecasts. Although inflation remains above the RBA’s 2%–3% target range, markets sharply scaled back bets on another rate hike this year to around 50% from more than 90% before the release. The softer readings also reinforced the view that the central bank is unlikely to resume policy tightening at its August 11 meeting. Still, the RBA's governor recently warned that another rate hike may still be needed to bring inflation back to target.

New Zealand Dollar Range-Bound — The New Zealand dollar traded around $0.578, lacking clear direction as investors awaited the US Federal Reserve's policy decision while monitoring developments in the Middle East. Although the consensus is for the Fed to hold rates steady, markets continue to price in roughly a one-third probability of a 25-bp rate hike. Meanwhile, geopolitical risks resurfaced after the US said it had intercepted Iranian missile attacks at forces based in the region, ending a days-long lull in hostilities and raising the risk of a broader conflict in the region. Investors are also looking ahead to New Zealand's business confidence and consumer sentiment data later this week for fresh clues on the economic outlook. The Reserve Bank of New Zealand is widely expected to deliver another 25-bp hike in September following hot inflation data last week, with markets implying rates to reach at least 3.0% by year-end and peak at 3.5% around mid-2027.

### 2026-07-29 10:13:52  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Aussie Dollar Dips on Softer CPI Data — The Australian dollar fell below $0.695, reaching its weakest level in two weeks as softer inflation data dampened expectations of additional interest rate hikes. Headline inflation unexpectedly eased to a four-month low of 3.8% in June from both May's reading and forecasts of 4.0%, while monthly consumer prices unexpectedly fell 0.1% for a second straight month. Meanwhile, the closely watched trimmed mean inflation rate rose 3.6% annually, below expectations of 3.7%, while quarterly core inflation increased 0.8%, also undershooting forecasts. The softer-than-expected readings reinforced expectations that the Reserve Bank of Australia will remain cautious on further policy tightening, although inflation remains above its 2%–3% target range. Markets sharply scaled back expectations for another RBA rate hike this year, with the implied probability falling to around 50% from more than 90% before the release.

### 2026-07-29 10:09:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Reverse Repo Rate #Importance 1

PBoC Maintains Liquidity Support Before Month-End — The People's Bank of China (PBoC) injected CNY 806.5 billion into the banking system through reverse repo operations on Wednesday, July 29, 2026, to maintain ample liquidity at month-end. The central bank supplied CNY 206.5 billion via seven-day reverse repos at an unchanged interest rate of 1.40%, while adding another CNY 600 billion through overnight reverse repos without disclosing the borrowing cost. The move followed the PBoC's announcement last week that it would conduct overnight reverse repo operations from July 29 to 31 and again on August 3, injecting a total of CNY 2.1 trillion through the short-term liquidity tool. The operations reflect the central bank's continued efforts to stabilize money market conditions and ensure sufficient cash in the banking system during a period of typically elevated funding demand at the end of the month.

### 2026-07-29 10:06:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Stock Market #Importance 1

China Stocks Fall on Middle East Jitters — The Shanghai Composite fell 0.4% to 3,798 on Wednesday, while the Shenzhen Component dropped 1.5% to 13,313, as investors weighed escalating Middle East tensions against a rebound in battered technology stocks. Tensions in the region intensified after Iran launched ballistic missiles toward US positions following a brief period of relative calm, though US Central Command said all projectiles were intercepted. Banking shares were among the biggest drags, led by Industrial and Commercial Bank of China (-1.0%), Agricultural Bank of China (-1.0%), and Bank of China (-1.3%). In contrast, technology stocks rebounded from recent losses, with Zhongji Innolight (1.6%), Eoptolink Technology (1.3%), NAURA Technology (1.1%), and Cambricon Technologies (2.6%) advancing despite persistent concerns over elevated valuations and AI-related spending. Attention is now turning to the Politburo meeting later this week, where investors hope to gain fresh insight into China's economic policy agenda.

### 2026-07-29 10:02:08  #其他

【貴州省地質礦產局原黨委副書記陳代軍被“雙開“】中央紀委國家監委網站7月29日通報，據貴州省紀委監委訊息：經貴州省委批准，貴州省紀委監委對貴州省地質礦產局原黨委副書記陳代軍嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，陳代軍喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對抗組織審查；罔顧中央八項規定精神，收受可能影響公正執行公務的禮品禮金，借用管理服務物件車輛，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請和旅遊活動安排，由他人支付應由本人支付的費用；違背組織原則，隱瞞不報個人有關事項，違規為他人謀取人事利益；廉潔底線失守，違規從事營利活動，透過民間借貸獲取大額回報，違規為親屬謀取利益，搞權色、錢色交易；違反工作紀律，違規干預市場經濟活動；將公權力當作攫取私利的工具，大搞權錢交易，利用職務便利為他人謀取利益，並非法收受鉅額財物。陳代軍嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經貴州省紀委常委會會議研究並報貴州省委批准，決定給予陳代軍開除黨籍處分；由貴州省監委給予其開除公職處分；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

### 2026-07-29 10:01:48  #其他

【最高法明確：參加單位支援的全日制教育後不返崗 須擔違約責任】今天，最高人民法院釋出《最高人民法院關於事業單位工作人員脫產參加全日制學歷教育後違反服務期約定有關問題的批覆》，維護事業單位人事管理秩序，守護誠信契約精神，助力夯實人力資本，保障人才投資良性迴圈。《批覆》明確脫產參加全日制學歷教育的工作人員違反服務期約定，事業單位請求其依約承擔違約責任的，人民法院依法予以支援的規則，有利於事業單位聚焦關鍵崗位、緊缺專業、核心業務，暢通真正願意紮根崗位、潛心幹事的工作人員獲得提升素質的渠道，使學歷教育成為補齊能力短板、強化隊伍支撐的重要舉措。工作人員學成後違約離職，本質是違背誠信原則的失信行為，是對契約精神的漠視，對事業單位信任的透支。《批覆》對於工作人員的失信行為予以否定性評價，有助於樹立契約意識、責任意識與誠信意識，充分發揮工作人員脫產參加全日制學歷教育所承載的賦能人才、發展事業、服務公益的重要使命。《批覆》綜合考慮實際損失、已經履行的年限、過錯等因素，根據公平原則和誠信原則，確定違約工作人員應當承擔的責任，讓人才培養真正發揮作用，實現個人成長與事業發展同頻共振、互利共贏。

【加強冷凍飲品生產環節監督管理 市場監管總局公開徵求意見】為規範冷凍飲品生產許可，加強對冷凍飲品生產環節的監督管理，7月29日，市場監管總局就《冷凍飲品生產許可審查細則（徵求意見稿）》向社會公開徵求意見，為期30天。該徵求意見稿主要修訂了以下內容。 完善生產許可範圍。為滿足冷凍飲品行業日益發展的需求，在梳理冷凍飲品六種常見產品型別外，新增其他冷凍飲品型別，更加貼合行業高質量發展要求。明確不得以分裝方式生產冷凍飲品。 細化設施裝置要求。細化生產場所設定與劃分、裝置設施配套、裝置佈局和工藝流程等內容，強調各區域涵蓋範圍及分隔要求，明確企業應建立冷凍飲品專用冷庫，並在實時監控裝置的基礎上配備溫度超限報警裝置。 嚴格全過程汙染防控。新增生產各階段溫溼度監控要求、微生物監控要求、冷庫衛生管理要求以及產品冷鏈物流衛生要求，強化企業汙染防控能力。 明確食品安全制度要求。要求企業建立並落實採購管理與進貨查驗、生產過程管理、出廠檢驗、運輸交付、追溯、自查及召回等全鏈條食品安全管理制度，同時要求配套倉儲、清潔消毒相應制度。細化各項制度管理要求，既確保企業執行到位，也為生產許可審查提供明確參考依據。

### 2026-07-29 10:00:28  #MKT News #Market Regions #Japan & APAC #USDJPY #Impact bearish #DXY #XAUUSD #Impact bullish

【Japan minimum wages set to rise steadily, supporting Bank of Japan rate-hike path】An advisory panel to Japan’s Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare recommended on Tuesday raising the national average minimum hourly wage by 4.9% to ¥1,176 (about $7.18) for the current fiscal year. The ¥55-an-hour increase is the second-largest on record, though below last year’s 6.3% rise, would cover more than 50 million workers and is expected to be phased in from October after prefectures set specific rates. The wage gain supports the Bank of Japan’s effort to create a wage-driven demand–inflation virtuous cycle, strengthening the case for further tightening as a weaker yen raises import energy and food costs and squeezes household spending.

【央行：今起陸續開展總量超2萬億隔夜逆回購操作】今天起（29日）到7月31日，中國人民銀行將每天開展6000億元隔夜逆回購操作、8月3日將開展3000億元隔夜逆回購操作。隔夜逆回購是央行進行公開市場操作的重要工具，主要作用是向市場投放短期流動性。具體操作上，隔夜逆回購首先由央行向大型銀行等一級交易商買入有價證券，相當於央行向市場“借出錢”。第二天，交易商按約定價格再將證券從央行贖回，資金從市場流回央行，從而完成短期流動性調控。（央視新聞）

US 10-Year Yield Steadies Ahead of Fed Decision — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note steadied around 4.62% on Wednesday as investors awaited the Federal Reserve’s upcoming policy decision, with policymakers widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged. However, markets continue to price in about a one-third chance of a 25-basis-point rate hike, reflecting an unusually high level of uncertainty this close to a policy meeting compared with recent years. Traders also assign roughly an 80% probability to a rate increase in September, reinforcing expectations that borrowing costs could remain elevated. Investors also monitored rebounding oil prices and renewed inflation concerns after fresh hostilities flared in the Middle East. The US military said it had successfully intercepted what it described as a surprise Iranian attack targeting US troops stationed across the Middle East, reigniting regional tensions.

South Korean Shares Hit Over 3-Month Low — The benchmark KOSPI fell more than 2% to around 5,895 on Wednesday, extending its decline to the lowest level in over three months, as investors remained cautious over AI infrastructure spending, elevated semiconductor valuations, and intensifying competition from China's chip industry. Sentiment also remained subdued after SK hynix reported record second-quarter revenue and operating profit but missed analyst expectations, reinforcing concerns that AI-driven growth may be moderating despite continued strength in high-bandwidth memory demand. SK hynix plunged nearly 5%, alongside losses in SK Square (-3.6%), Samsung Electro-Mechanics (-3.8%), HD Hyundai Heavy Industries (-1.0%), and Hanwha Aerospace (-1.5%). Meanwhile, lower oil prices following easing Middle East supply concerns and selective bargain hunting after the previous session's 10.8% plunge provided only limited support to the broader market.

Australia Inflation Rate at 4-Month Low of 3.8% — The annual inflation rate in Australia unexpectedly eased to 3.8% in June 2026 from both May's reading and market expectations of 4.0%, but remained above the central bank's 2–3% target range. ### 2026-07-29 09:39:32  #市場 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【凱投宏觀：AI泡沫破裂或讓韓元在18個月內升值15%】據凱投宏觀(Capital Economics)稱，如果更廣泛的AI股票榮景破滅，那麼韓元未來18個月可能會走強。凱投宏觀市場經濟學家Elias Hilmer表示，AI泡沫破裂將為美元帶來兩大不利因素：美聯儲可能在2028年降息，以及支撐美股榮景的資本流入放緩。儘管半導體出口也會放緩，但韓國仍將繼續保持鉅額貿易順差。如果AI驅動的股市上漲行情結束，韓國散戶投資者的海外資金流出放緩，甚至轉為迴流，韓元目前的低估情況可能大幅修正。預計到2027年底，韓元將比目前水準上漲約5%。到2028年底，韓元可能升值約15%。短期內，隨著美聯儲立場趨於鷹派以及資本持續外流，韓元將繼續承壓。預計美聯儲到2027年初將加息三次，每次25個基點；至於韓國，AI出口熱潮預計將推動經濟維持強勁，貨幣政策風險偏向進一步緊縮。

### 2026-07-29 09:29:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Platinum Trades Near Multi-Month Lows — Platinum futures traded around $1,600 an ounce, near late-November lows, pressured by weakness across precious metals amid persistent hostilities in the Middle East. The conflict kept inflation risks in focus and reinforced expectations that interest rates could stay higher for longer, weighing on non-yielding assets such as platinum. Investors now await the Federal Reserve's policy decision, where rates are widely expected to remain unchanged. Still, markets see about a one-third chance of a rate hike this week and roughly an 80% chance of another increase in September. The platinum market was also weighed down by expectations for softer industrial and automotive demand despite the tight supply outlook. The ongoing shift toward electric vehicles, which do not require autocatalysts, has clouded demand prospects even as the platinum market is forecast to post a fourth straight annual supply deficit due to constrained mine supply and declining above-ground inventories.

### 2026-07-29 09:22:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Gasoline Rises on Renewed US-Iran Hostilities — US gasoline prices rose to around $3.37 per gallon, moving back toward a two-month high, as renewed tensions between Iran and the US revived concerns over energy supply disruptions. Iran fired ballistic missiles at US positions in the Middle East, escalating the conflict after a temporary lull, though the US Central Command reported that all projectiles were intercepted. The US also carried out operations alongside Saudi forces in Iraq against Iran-backed militant groups. Meanwhile, Iran rejected Oman’s proposal to share oversight of shipping lanes in the Strait of Hormuz, demanding control over the inbound route and a portion of outbound traffic. Outside the region, Russia eased gasoline purchase restrictions in several areas as supply conditions improved, but the extension of its export ban through year-end continued to limit relief for global gasoline availability. Elsewhere, industry data showed gasoline inventories rose by 918,000 barrels in the week ending July 24.

### 2026-07-29 09:14:49  #市場 #央行 #國際

【日元持續下跌 考驗植田和男安撫市場的能力】本週日元將面臨一個關鍵的風險時刻，日本央行行長植田和男將在週五央行政策會議後發表講話。儘管普遍預期日本央行將維持利率不變，但日元交易員將密切關注植田的講話，以防任何可能引發日元進一步下跌的失誤。日元兌美元在6月份下跌至自1986年以來最弱水平，並且本月繼續走弱。日元持續受到油價上漲，對財政政策的擔憂，以及利率差異寬闊的壓力。儘管植田釋放出更清晰的訊號可能有助於穩定日元，但市場參與者提醒稱，這不會引發日元大幅上漲。“如果植田的立場不夠強硬，那麼日元兌美元匯率將繼續走弱，跌破165。”RBC Bluebay首席投資官Mark Dowding表示，“我認為植田會在9月或10月的會議上為加息敞開大門”，但他不太可能足夠鷹派，從而導致日元大幅上漲。

【上半年居民消費物流保持韌性增長 線上消費是核心動力】中國物流與採購聯合會今天（29日）公佈今年上半年物流執行資料。從民生消費物流需求來看，上半年，單位與居民物品物流總額保持韌性增長，呈現線上業態支撐、消費升級引領、下沉市場擴容的積極特徵。上半年，單位與居民物品物流需求同比增長3.8%。其中，線上消費是核心增長動力，上半年全國實物商品網上零售額同比增長4.8%，增速高於同期商品零售額3.7個百分點，電商快遞業務同步擴容，新業態、新模式持續為民生物流注入增長動能。消費升級推動需求結構最佳化，上半年綠色智慧、品質消費類物流需求穩步釋放，限額以上單位通訊器材類商品零售額同比增長14.4%，高能效等級家電零售額增速超30%，化妝品、潮流玩具等品類保持較快增勢，帶動末端物流提質升級。另外，下沉市場拓展增長空間，上半年鄉村消費品零售額同比增長2.5%，增速快於城鎮1.3個百分點；縣鄉物流配送體系不斷完善，持續釋放民生消費物流增量。（央視新聞）

【金融危機預警者Whitney預計美聯儲按兵不動 美國經濟第四季面臨考驗】 曾因在2007年全球金融危機前夕率先發出警告而聲名鵲起的前銀行分析師Meredith Whitney表示，隨著世界盃帶來的一次性經濟提振效應以及財政支出餘溫逐漸消退，美國經濟將在第四季度迎來一場“清算時刻”。
Whitney週二接受媒體採訪時表示，每週信用卡餘額這一反映消費者支出而非迴圈債務的實時指標，目前增速已較5月放緩。與此同時，美國消費者也正在消化汽油價格上漲帶來的壓力。Meredith Whitney Advisory Group創始人兼執行長Whitney表示，上述因素將為美聯儲週三維持利率不變提供理由。她駁斥了Citadel Securities等機構提出的觀點，即意外加息將有助於提高美聯儲主席凱文·沃什的公信力。Whitney認為，沃什設立五個政策工作組的做法，實際上為其爭取了數個月時間，無需急於作出政策轉向決定。“我認為他們會維持利率不變，同時繼續使用偏強硬、偏鷹派的措辭，為未來行動保留迴旋空間，”她表示。談及沃什時，Whitney稱：“他的傾向是等待，並確保自己真的做出了正確判斷。”

Dollar Steadies Ahead of Fed Policy Decision — The dollar index held steady around 101.3 on Wednesday after a volatile start to the week, as investors awaited the Federal Reserve’s upcoming policy decision, where it is expected to leave interest rates unchanged. However, markets continue to price in about a one-third chance of a 25-basis-point rate hike, reflecting an unusually high level of uncertainty this close to a decision compared with recent years. Traders also see roughly an 80% probability of a rate increase in September, reinforcing expectations that borrowing costs could stay elevated. Meanwhile, investors also monitored rebounding oil prices and renewed inflation concerns after fresh hostilities flared in the Middle East. The US military said it had successfully intercepted what it described as a surprise Iranian attack targeting US troops stationed across the Middle East, reigniting regional tensions.

【五糧液普五八代領跌 白酒大單品總價創近五天以來新低】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，中國白酒市場主要大單品的終端零售總價7月29日再度小幅回撥。如果主要單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為9886元，較昨日下跌12元，在昨日小幅反彈後再度轉弱，創五天以來最低，但比7月18日創下的階段低點仍高出81元。縱觀全場，過去一天酒市終端交投情緒環比降溫，空方再佔上風，低位修復節奏再受擾動。

今日11大白酒單品三漲六跌兩平，輸家佔據明顯優勢。上漲方面，飛天茅臺、精品茅臺與青花郎均上漲2元/瓶。其中，飛天茅臺的全國零售均價終結此前連續七日小幅回撥，自7.18提價後高位區間止跌回升；精品茅臺昨日回落後今日小幅反彈；青花郎連續兩日走升，續創近一個月最高紀錄。下跌方面，五糧液普五八代跌幅居前，下跌7元/瓶，近期低位寬幅震盪格局延續；青花汾20與古井貢古20均下跌3元/瓶，其中前者平近一個月最低價紀錄，後者近期價格漲跌反覆，短線反彈屢受壓制；五糧液1618與習酒君品均下跌2元/瓶，其中前者連續兩日走低，後者結束昨日反彈，仍處於近一個月高位區間；國窖1573小跌1元/瓶，近一個月價格中樞明顯下移。洋河夢之藍M6+與水晶劍南春價格環比昨日保持不變，其中前者連續兩日維持在600元/瓶，後者連續兩日維持在402元/瓶。

【銀河證券：機構大幅減持 有色行業基金持倉回落至近年低點】銀河證券A股有色金屬行業2026Q2基金持倉分析指出，2026年二季度受中東衝突升級、霍爾木茲海峽封鎖影響，油價大幅衝高帶來通脹壓力，市場對美聯儲政策預期由年初的降息轉為加息，市場流動性與經濟預期的改變使有色金屬未來價格走勢與有色金屬企業未來業績的不確定性加大，主動權益類公募基金在2026年二季度大幅減持了銅、鋁、黃金等此前超配的有色金屬子行業，並在市場風格的帶動下加倉了與AI概念相關的鎢、鎳、磁性金屬等小金屬細分板塊。綜合來看，2026年二季度主動權益類公募基金對A股有色金屬行業重倉持股市值佔股票投資市值比達到了1.98%，環比2026年一季度的3.22%下行1.24個百分點，在2024年四季度後連續兩個季度減持有色金屬行業，且2026年二季度主動權益類公募基金對A股有色金屬行業重倉持股的單季度減持幅度創下了2010年以來的新高。

【澳大利亞研發AI蚊子探測器 可快速精準識別致病蚊種】澳大利亞伍倫貢大學研究人員研發出一款智慧監測裝置，透過捕捉並分析蚊子振翅聲響，快速精準識別具有致病風險的蚊種。該裝置由基蘭·特里維迪博士研發，已在聯合國人工智慧向善全球峰會上展出。裝置依託人工智慧技術，可識別伊蚊、按蚊與庫蚊等全球危害最嚴重的3類致病蚊種。不同蚊種的振翅頻率存在細微差異，形成獨一無二的“聲紋”，裝置可透過精準分析振翅頻率，在數秒內快速區分不同蚊種。裝置搭載微型機器學習技術，適合在鄉村及偏遠地區推廣使用。該裝置的核心是基於Arduino平臺的一塊微型電路板，配備了麥克風與顯示屏。研發團隊利用開源的蚊子振翅音訊資料對AI模型進行訓練，目前該裝置的蚊種識別準確率已達到88%。（環球時報）

EU Natural Gas Rebounds — European natural gas prices jumped over 4% to above €58 per MWh on Wednesday, rebounding after two consecutive sessions of losses, as fresh hostilities in the Middle East deepened concerns over prolonged supply disruptions. The US military said on Tuesday it had intercepted multiple missiles launched by Iran, ending a days-long lull in fighting between the two sides. Both nations had largely held fire over the weekend following nearly two weeks of nightly US strikes on Iran and repeated attacks by Tehran targeting Washington's allies across the Gulf. The latest escalation worsens Europe’s gas supply outlook as the region races to replenish storage ahead of the winter heating season. Supply concerns were further exacerbated by reports that QatarEnergy, one of the world's largest LNG exporters, has extended its force majeure for European buyers until at least the end of September.

### 2026-07-29 08:26:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Food Inflation #Importance 1

Japan Food Sales Tax Set at 1% Under Takaichi’s Proposal — Japan plans to temporarily cut its sales tax on food and drinks to 1% for two years, Bloomberg reported. The government chose a low positive rate instead of zero to avoid the costly, time-consuming reprogramming of retailers’ cash registers; a 0% rate would take about a year to implement, while 1% could halve that timeline. To take effect in April 2027, the measure must still be legislated and passed by the Diet (parliament), with draft legislation expected in an extraordinary session in autumn 2026. Policymakers hope the measure will ease household burdens, though economists warned that prices may not fall as much as expected, limiting the benefit for consumers.

Silver Holds Decline as Oil Prices Rebound — Silver traded near $57 an ounce on Wednesday, holding the previous session’s decline as oil prices rebounded following renewed hostilities in the Middle East, reviving geopolitical tensions and keeping investors focused on inflationary pressures and the interest rate outlook. The US military said it had successfully intercepted what it described as a surprise Iranian attack targeting US troops stationed across the Middle East. Investors also awaited the Federal Reserve’s policy decision, where it is expected to leave interest rates unchanged. However, markets continue to price in about a one-third chance of a rate hike, reflecting an unusually high level of uncertainty this close to a policy announcement despite repeated calls from President Donald Trump for lower interest rates. Traders also assign roughly an 80% probability to a rate increase in September, reinforcing expectations that borrowing costs could remain elevated.

### 2026-07-29 07:51:43  #MKT News #Commodities

【CITIC Construction Investment Research: Weak expectations, strong reality — lithium prices likely to sustain rebound】CITIC Construction Investment research says lithium has over-discounted prospective loosening; weekly inventories continue to fall and prices are oversold in the near term. Fundamentals remain strong for the year and supply stays tight: Baichuan data show several lithium-salt plants have announced maintenance, creating phased output cuts this month and next that should tighten short-term supply; heavy snow in South American salt lakes may further hit Q3 production and shipments. Demand has not softened through the July–August off-season—demand appears front‑loaded and downstream rigid procurement is supporting prices. Maintenance-driven supply reductions plus robust demand are continuing to draw inventories, underpinning a price floor and raising the odds of stronger prices into September–October.

【英國智庫NIESR小幅上調經濟增長預期，警告通脹將持續至2029年】英國一家主要智庫在央行公佈利率決議前一天釋出的預測顯示，儘管伊朗戰爭引發能源價格衝擊，但英國經濟展現的韌性超出預期；不過通脹率在2029年之前，預計將持續高於央行的目標水平。英國國家經濟社會研究院(NIESR)週三表示，預計2026年國內生產總值（GDP）同比將增長1.1%，高於4月預測的0.9%增幅。“今年上半年，英國經濟展現出令人意外的韌性，但還是會出現增長放緩。即使中東局勢相對迅速恢復和平，通脹仍將上升，新任財政大臣將需要做出一些艱難的決定，”NIESR宏觀經濟副主任Stephen Millard表示。該機構預計2026年平均通脹率為3.1%，高於6月預測的2.6%。通脹率預計在2027年2月達到3.8%的峰值，低於4月預測的4.1%。通脹率要到2029年才能回到目標水平，比此前預測晚一年。

【Guan Tao: hawkish Fed official's deeper intent may still favour rate cuts】Guan Tao, global chief economist at BOC Securities, wrote that Fed disagreement over US inflation and rate policy remains, roughly split into three camps: dovish (Williams), neutral (Waller) and hawkish. A hawkish Fed official who testified to the Senate Banking Committee on July 15 repeatedly voiced dissatisfaction with inflation, reiterated a zero-tolerance stance and said the Fed would continue to push inflation back to target. Guan says the official may be staking out a hawkish posture now to build credibility and anchor inflation expectations, but his push to revise the inflation framework and his long-run view that AI will exert disinflationary pressure suggest his deeper intent may still lean toward eventual rate cuts. The official even said he is more concerned with the digits to the left of the decimal point of inflation. Guan therefore sees the Fed likely to keep policy unchanged through the year; however, if US financial turmoil, recession or labour-market deterioration occur over the next two years, the Fed could restart cuts. Current market pricing for additional hikes may be excessive.

【證券時報：AI重塑經濟執行邏輯 專家呼籲堅持“投資於人”】對於AI對宏觀經濟產生的影響，CF40釋出的2026年二季度宏觀政策季度報告指出，AI既可能透過替代和增強智力勞動、激發上下游技術創新來提高生產率，也可能因為互補性投資、組織重構和關鍵投入約束，使生產率紅利呈現滯後釋放的特徵。 目前，AI投資仍處於快速上升期，圍繞算力、資料中心和軟體等方面的大規模投資，將帶來新的庫存週期。CF40資深研究員、中國社科院世界經濟與政治研究所副所長張斌指出，AI需要鉅額、長期的重資本投資，在新的庫存週期、固定資產投資週期以及資產價格波動等因素作用下，大規模的AI投資將帶來內生性的、壓力更大的週期性需求衝擊。 北京大學國家發展研究院院長黃益平認為，當前AI並未顯著提升全要素生產率，這屬於技術落地的短期滯後現象。隨著產業調整、資源最佳化配置逐步完成，AI將成為經濟增長的核心動力。 著眼於進一步推動AI創新與應用，黃益平表示，應堅持“以人為本”的原則，實施“投資於人”的戰略。培養掌握“專業領域+AI工具”的複合型人才，促進“人機協同”；完善失業保險與兜底社會保障，建立覆蓋生命全週期、健康全過程的優質高效護理服務體系。

【我國將組織實施新一輪油氣勘探開發戰略行動】 國家能源局近日釋出的《中國油氣勘探開發發展報告（2026）》顯示，2019—2025年，我國油氣行業大力提升油氣勘探開發力度“七年行動計劃”圓滿收官。為落實增儲上產長期要求，實現油氣核心需求自主保障，2026年油氣行業將組織實施新一輪油氣勘探開發戰略行動，持續加大勘探開發投入，確保“十五五”開好局、起好步，實現全年國內原油產量穩定在2億噸以上、天然氣產量持續提升。《報告》顯示，2019—2025年，全國新增億噸級油田17個、千億立方米級氣田34個；年均新增探明地質儲量石油14億噸、天然氣1.5萬億立方米以上，較2018年分別增長了55.0%和58.7%。其中，頁岩油氣、煤層氣階段新增探明地質儲量佔比達22%，成為重要戰略接替資源。七年間，我國累計新建原油產能1.5億噸，實現原油產量企穩回升，2025年增產達2.16億噸，創歷史新高。（科技日報）

【SK海力士財報不及預期 美股盤後一度大跌9％】SK海力士(SKHY.O)週三公佈財報，受科技巨頭加大AI資料中心投入、帶動先進儲存晶片需求強勁的影響，公司第二季度營業利潤飆升557％至60.5萬億韓元（約合416.2億美元），重新整理歷史紀錄，上年同期為9.2萬億韓元。然而這一數字低於市場預期的64萬億韓元，主要因其高階儲存晶片（HBM）佔比較競爭對手更高，反而未能充分受益於本輪常規儲存晶片的強勁漲價週期。這加劇了人們對推動半導體行業發展的AI熱潮可能正在放緩的擔憂。SK海力士營收也未達預期，財報顯示，其第二季度收入為79萬億韓元，市場預期為84萬億韓元。受財報影響，SK海力士在美股週二收跌9％後，盤後一度再跌9％，閃迪(SNDK.O)和美光科技(MU.O)也跟跌超4％，抹去希捷科技(STX.O)財報帶來的提振。

### 2026-07-29 07:08:35  #公司 #市場 #國際

【受人工智慧晶片需求推動，SK海力士第二季度利潤增長六倍，但未達預期】韓國晶片製造商SK海力士 週三公佈，受大型科技公司加大對人工智慧資料中心的投入推動，對先進記憶體晶片的需求強勁，其季度營業利潤增長了六倍多，創下歷史新高。這家英偉達的供應商公佈，4月至6月期間的營業利潤為60.5萬億韓元(（416.2億美元) ），而去年同期為9.2萬億韓元。但這一資料未達到LSEG SmartEstimate預估的64萬億韓元，該機構在預測中更側重於那些預測準確率更高、更穩定的分析師。SK海力士在宣告中表示：“受人工智慧基礎設施投資擴張帶來的持續需求增長推動，用於人工智慧伺服器的高效能產品引領了價格上漲，使公司業績超越了上一季度創下的歷史紀錄。”SK海力士未能達到分析師預期，是因為該公司在AI資料中心所用高階記憶體晶片領域的業務佔比高於競爭對手，這意味著它在傳統記憶體晶片價格強勁上漲時獲益較少。該公司表示，其季度營收增長257%，達到79.3萬億韓元。

### 2026-07-29 07:04:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Chile Holds Interest Rate at 4.5% — The Central Bank of Chile kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 4.5% in a unanimous decision at its July meeting, as widely expected. Policymakers said the macroeconomic outlook remains subject to greater-than-usual uncertainty as renewed fighting between the US and Iran pushed oil prices back toward $100 per barrel and clouded the outlook for global supply. Domestically, economic activity weakened more than expected, with May's Imacec falling 0.9% year-on-year while investment and labor market conditions softened. Meanwhile, annual inflation accelerated to 4.3% in June, slightly above forecasts, as core inflation rose faster than expected and short-term inflation expectations increased alongside higher oil prices. However, inflation expectations two years ahead remained anchored at 3%. The board reiterated that it will assess policy meeting by meeting and take the measures needed to return inflation to its 3% target over a two-year horizon.

NZX 50 Hits Fresh Record High in Morning Trade — New Zealand's stocks climbed 15 points, or 0.1%, to 13,877 in Wednesday morning trade, supported by gains in real estate, energy, consumer discretionary stocks, financials, and communication services. The broader index hit a new intraday record high after closing at a record high of 13,862 a day earlier, marking its second consecutive record close. Traders tried to maintain momentum amid easing oil prices, as easing inflation concerns reduced expectations of further interest rate hikes. However, traders remained cautious ahead of the Fed's monetary policy decision and China's PMI data, as well as the release of New Zealand's consumer confidence and business outlook data for July, due later this week. 【Goldman Sachs says Japan AI sell-off presents buying opportunity】Goldman Sachs says the sharp sell-off in Japan AI-related stocks has created a buy opportunity and that strong earnings could revive interest in semiconductor names. Bruce Kirk, Goldman's chief Japan equity strategist, said the correction has lowered the earnings bar, making outperformance more price-moving, and that current levels look attractive to add positions absent a material deterioration in geopolitical risk. He added that earnings will ultimately decide the direction and that strong results at much lower price levels should refocus investors on the AI profit story. Goldman cautioned short-term volatility may persist: its prime-brokerage data show hedge funds' gross and net Japan exposures sit above the 98th percentile of their five-year ranges, a historically high level.

### 2026-07-29 05:22:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #API Crude Oil Stock Change #Importance 1

US Crude Oil Inventories Unexpectedly Rise — US crude oil inventories unexpectedly increased by 3.3 million barrels in the week ending July 24, defying market expectations for a 2.5 million-barrel draw. The build followed a 2.6 million-barrel increase in the previous week. Despite the recent gains, commercial crude inventories excluding the Strategic Petroleum Reserve (SPR) have declined by more than 54 million barrels over the past 15 weeks and remain about 3 million barrels lower year to date. Another 3.7 million barrels were withdrawn from the SPR during the week, reducing holdings to 307.7 million barrels, just above the widely accepted operational minimum range of 250-300 million barrels. At the Cushing, Oklahoma hub, inventories fell by 273,000 barrels after a 737,000-barrel decline the previous week. US crude production edged down to 13.80 million bpd from 13.86 million bpd. Meanwhile, gasoline inventories rose by 918,000 barrels, while distillate stockpiles fell by 125,000 barrels.

Ibovespa Gains as BCB Rate Cut Bets Grow — The Ibovespa rose 0.7% to close at 176,565 on Tuesday after Brazil's mid-July inflation report came in below expectations. Annual inflation slowed to 4.52%, below forecasts of 4.67% and down from 4.8% in June. The softer reading, together with June's lower-than-expected inflation data, reinforced expectations of a 25-basis-point Selic rate cut next week. Financials led the gains, with Banco do Brasil up 1.6% and B3 adding 1.8%. Bradesco (+0.3%) and Santander (+2.2%) are due to report earnings tomorrow. Utilities also rose, with Axia rising 1.9%. Ambev gained 1.5% ahead of its earnings release on Thursday. WEG extended gains, rising 0.6% after Goldman Sachs upgraded the stock to Neutral from Sell, citing more balanced market expectations. In contrast, Vivo (-6.3%) and TIM (-5.8%) tumbled after reporting results. TIM's mobile service revenue growth slowed to its weakest pace since 2021, while Vivo saw an 8% increase in depreciation and amortization expenses.

### 2026-07-29 04:17:21  #國際

【中方：政府間談判是討論安理會改革問題唯一合法平臺】中國常駐聯合國代表團臨時代辦孫磊28日在聯大透過安理會改革政府間談判過渡決定後發言，指出政府間談判是討論安理會改革問題的唯一合法平臺。孫磊說，中方歡迎聯大以協商一致方式透過口頭決定，將本屆聯大安理會改革政府間談判技術性過渡到下屆聯大。政府間談判是討論安理會改革問題的唯一合法平臺。安理會改革事關各國重大利益。中方希望各方秉持戰略視野和長遠眼光，共同維護政府間談判主渠道地位，堅持會員國共識原則，在政府間談判繼續深入溝通、凝聚共識，確保改革程序朝著提升發展中國家的代表性和發言權的唯一正確方向發展，不斷行穩致遠。孫磊表示，中方願同各方一道，繼續推動安理會改革朝著符合會員國共同利益和聯合國事業健康發展的方向前進，為推動構建更加公正合理的全球治理體系作出積極貢獻。（新華社）

### 2026-07-29 04:13:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Mixed as Chip Selloff Deepens — US equity indices closed mixed on Tuesday as chipmakers extended their selloff, while strength in software and defensive sectors supported the broader market. The S&P 500 gained 0.2%, the Nasdaq 100 fell 1%, and the Dow rose 537 points. Concerns over slowing AI infrastructure spending and the circular nature of data center investment weighed on compute and memory chipmakers. Micron slumped 8.8%, AMD lost 8.1%, Intel fell 5.9%, and Sandisk tumbled 14.2%. The sector also came under pressure after the strong IPO of China's CXMT in Shanghai boosted optimism over rising competition. Meanwhile, software and non-tech stocks outperformed, with Microsoft up 1.1% and Meta closing little changed ahead of earnings on Wednesday. Lower oil prices and renewed US signals favoring diplomacy with Iran eased credit costs and supported the broader market. Berkshire Hathaway gained 3.1%, while Visa rose 1.1% ahead of its earnings release. Coca-Cola jumped 7.1% after raising its full-year outlook.

US Dollar Eases from 15-Month High — The dollar index softened to 101.3 on Tuesday after testing a 15-month high of 101.6 in the previous session as softer energy prices limited the outlook of a rate hike by the Federal Reserve this year. US officials stressed that a diplomatic resolution may still be achieved with Iran in their war, driving fuel prices to ease off their local peaks and trimming the risk of a rebound in energy inflation. Still, fuel prices remained elevated in a historical level as exports from the Middle East are still low. A portion of FOMC members had recently signaled that higher inflation and evidence of a robust labor market could warrant higher rates. The Federal Reserve is expected to hold its policy rates unchanged tomorrow, but rate futures continued reflect the consensus of a hike this year. The greenback held recent gains against the euro and the yen, although political volatility in the UK maintained the sterling's recent appreciation.

FTSE 100 Rises to 5-Month High — The FTSE 100 advanced 0.9% on Monday, rising above 10,800 to its highest level since the record close on February 27, supported by strong performances from consumer goods and healthcare stocks. Unilever shares surged more than 8% after the company reported better-than-expected results, driven by solid growth in the US and emerging markets, and raised its 2026 outlook. GSK also gained around 4% after delivering an earnings beat, announcing cost-cutting measures and plans to reinvest savings into drug research, while maintaining that operating profit growth should reach the upper end of its guidance. In contrast, Barclays shares dropped nearly 6% despite reporting strong second-quarter investment banking results, upgrading its annual income target, and announcing a £1 billion share buyback. Investors compared the performance unfavourably with stronger results from US banks. Meanwhile, oil majors Shell and BP declined more than 1% each as crude prices continued to weaken.

### 2026-07-28 23:49:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

CAC 40 Rises on Strong Corporate Earnings — The CAC 40 rose 0.6% to close at 8,459 on Tuesday as investors welcomed a fresh batch of corporate earnings. LVMH gained 1% after reporting stronger-than-expected first-half revenue and earnings and an acceleration in second-quarter sales growth. The upbeat results lifted the luxury sector, with L'Oréal up 2.4%, Hermès rising 2.3%, and Kering adding 2.9%. Safran advanced 2.3% after raising its full-year guidance on stronger-than-expected first-half results, while Orange surged 5.3% after posting solid first-half earnings and upgrading its outlook. In contrast, Michelin fell 1.5% after reporting revenue and net profit below expectations. On the macro front, French consumer confidence improved to 86 in July, the highest level in four months, up from 84 in June, though it remained below the long-term average of 100. Air Liquide, Essilor, Kering, Orange, and Safran are due to report earnings on Wednesday.

【Zhongji Xuchuang: 1.6T ASP far above market rumours; no destructive price competition】Zhongji Xuchuang told investors its 1.6T ASP is well above market rumours and the industry is not engaging in cutthroat price competition. Overall demand is very strong, raw materials are scarce and 1.6T deliveries are tight; only a few vendors can deliver 1.6T at scale. The company says its pricing is competitive and can sustain current gross margins. Nearly all customer orders already cover full-year 2026, with some orders extending into 2027 and delivery schedules broken down monthly. For 2027 the company sees clear, high-certainty demand and rapid growth for 800G, 1.6T, 2.4T and NPO products; several key customers have issued sizeable new-product guidance for 2028. Zhongji Xuchuang expects downstream key customers’ capex to remain robust and customer investment in the sector to continue rising.

### 2026-07-28 23:34:25  #MKT News #Commodities #Precious Metals #XAUUSD #Impact mixed

【Reuters survey: analysts lower gold forecasts for first time since end-2023; central bank buying seen cushioning decline】A Reuters survey of 29 analysts and traders over the past three weeks shows forecasters trimmed gold price forecasts for the first time since end-2023 after a sharp retreat from January’s record high. The median 2026 forecast is $4,509/oz, down from $4,916 three months earlier and marking the first quarterly downgrade in 11 quarters. The 2027 forecast averaged $4,610/oz, versus $5,100 in the prior survey. Most respondents still expect central bank buying and fiscal-sustainability concerns to provide support. Gold hit a record $5,595/oz in January but plunged in Q2 as the Iran war intensified energy-driven inflation and lifted rate-hike expectations, producing the worst quarter since 2013; spot gold is down about 22% since the conflict began.

【受颱風“紅霞”影響的廣東使用者已全部恢復供電】28日從南方電網瞭解到，截至18時，受今年第12號颱風“紅霞”影響的廣東使用者已全部恢復供電。26日凌晨，颱風“紅霞”的中心在廣東省惠州市惠東縣平海鎮登陸，登陸時中心附近最大風力14級（45米/秒）。颱風登陸時，部分電力設施遭颱風損毀，不少居民家中出現斷電情況。隨後，電力部門組織搶險力量赴臺風登陸點開展搶修工作。據電力部門介紹，在臺風襲擊廣東期間，惠州、汕尾等地50餘萬戶使用者用電受到影響。廣東電網公司在其供電營業區組建了200餘支搶險隊伍，奮戰60餘小時，目前已全部恢復供電。深圳供電局派出搶險人員900餘人次，奔赴受颱風影響較嚴重的深汕特別合作區開展搶修工作，順利恢復當地供電。（新華社）

### 2026-07-28 23:02:03  #公司 #國際

【全球最大單批次綠氨出口啟航 中國綠色燃料獲國際認可】7月28日訊息，國家電投集團電投綠能3750噸“氫洲·綠氨”今天從連雲港離岸起航發往韓國，此次出海創下全球最大單批次綠氨出口紀錄，也是中國綠色燃料首次憑藉全生命週期碳減排貢獻，獲得國際市場綠色價值認可。本次出口的綠氨產品，全部產自國家電投集團“氫洲·大安綠氨”示範專案。該專案作為國家清潔低碳氫能示範工程、戰略性新興產業“百大工程”，2025年7月建成投產，配套新能源裝機80萬千瓦，可年產綠氫3.2萬噸、綠氨18萬噸，一舉斬獲全球最大單體綠氨裝置等五項“全球之最”。“氫洲·大安綠氨”示範專案2025年10月獲得全球首張“非生物來源可再生燃料氨（RFNBO Ammonia）”認證。本批次出口綠氨首次以“全生命週期碳減排貢獻”獲得國際市場綠色價值認可。經測算，3750噸綠氨相較國內傳統煤制合成氨，全生命週期可減排二氧化碳超萬噸，等效於約100萬棵樹木一年的固碳量。當前，國際海事組織碳強度指標趨嚴、歐盟碳排放交易體系全面覆蓋航運領域，全球可溯源、可核查的綠色燃料市場需求持續攀升。本次規模化出口，標誌著中國綠氨產品正式深度嵌入全球綠色燃料供應鏈。（新浪科技）

### 2026-07-28 23:02:01  #央行 #美聯儲

【美國7月消費者信心下降 民眾對營商環境和就業市場看法惡化】美國7月消費者信心下降，民眾對當前商業環境和勞動力市場的看法惡化。世界大型企業聯合會週二公佈的資料顯示，消費者信心指數下降1.4點至90.8，前一個月資料則獲向上修正。經濟學家預估中值為92.4。衡量當前狀況的指標降至2021年以來最低，而反映對未來六個月預期的指標持平。汽油和食品價格高企可能對美國家庭造成壓力，進一步凸顯市場對通脹持續和生活成本上升的廣泛擔憂。與此同時，在春季表現強勁後，招聘活動已出現放緩跡象。世界大型企業聯合會的調查顯示，7月消費者對就業市場的看法轉弱。認為職位充足的受訪者比例降至24.6%，認為工作難找的比例也小幅下降。這兩個比例之間的差值是經濟學家密切關注的指標，7月收窄至2021年以來最小，表明求職者面臨的勞動力市場環境較為艱難。本次調查覆蓋7月1日至22日。其間，美國加油站汽油價格先降至3月以來最低，隨後因美伊衝突再度升級、油價上漲而反彈。美國上週末暫停空襲後，市場情緒趨穩，油價回落。

### 2026-07-28 22:45:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Dallas Fed Services Index #Importance 1

Dallas Fed Business Activity Rises Most in 11 Months — The general business activity index for the service sector published by the Federal Reserve Bank of Dallas rose to 6.6 in July of 2026 from 2.9 in the previous month, marking the sharpest pace of growth in 11 months. The result was aligned with other similar surveys in the period as exports of energy in the Middle East were momentarily restored, lowering energy prices and operating costs to improve margin prospects in the sector. Revenue growth eased slightly (9.8 vs 9.5 in June), although hours worked rose faster (4.9 vs 3.6). Meanwhile, input prices slowed (33.5 vs 36.5) while selling charges rose sharply (14.2 vs 7.7). Looking ahead, the general business conditions for companies rose to 21 from 17.1.

【AI 時尚新職業爆發 部分AI時尚從業者月薪能達3萬元】現在，AI已經滲透到時尚行業的設計、生產、營銷、零售全鏈條，催生出一批全新工種。 一是創意設計類，比如給虛擬人物設計服飾的虛擬時裝設計師、AI時尚內容設計師。 二是資料運營類，如AI趨勢分析師和數字穿搭師。 三是技術適配類，如3D虛擬樣衣師和AI設計訓練師。 AI催生出與時尚相關的新崗位，薪資水平也呈現明顯溢價。資料顯示，國內虛擬時裝設計師應屆畢業生月薪一般在1.2萬元左右，3-5年資深從業者月薪可達3萬元；數字穿搭師薪資區間一般為8000元到1.8萬元/月。行業資料顯示，2026年全球人工智慧在時尚市場的價值將達到9.7億美元，預計到2035年將達到47.9億美元。AI產生的影響不侷限在服裝行業，也溢位到時尚買手、陳列師等崗位，如今從業者可藉助AI完成基礎資料整理、趨勢初判、陳列初稿搭建等工作。 （央視財經）

### 2026-07-28 22:35:36  #焦點 #國際

【商務部條約法律司負責人就世貿組織釋出我訴土耳其電動汽車和其他車輛限制措施世貿爭端案專家組報告答記者問】
有記者問：日內瓦時間7月28日，世貿組織公佈了中國訴土耳其電動汽車和其他車輛限制措施世貿爭端案專家組報告。請問中方對此有何評論？ 

答：我們注意到，世貿組織散發了中國訴土耳其電動汽車和其他車輛限制措施案（案件編號：DS629）專家組報告。專家組支援中方主張，裁定土方涉案措施違反世貿規則。中方對此裁決表示歡迎。

2023年以來，土方對中國出口的電動汽車、混動汽車等車輛，先後採取附加關稅、進口許可證等限制措施，違反約束關稅、國民待遇、最惠國待遇等多邊貿易規則，嚴重損害中方正當貿易利益和中土經貿關係。中方敦促土方尊重世貿組織專家組裁決，採取切實行動，儘快糾正違規措施。

同時，我想指出，中方堅定奉行互利共贏的開放戰略，堅定維護以世貿組織為核心的多邊貿易體制。中方願在符合多邊貿易規則的基礎上，與各方不斷提升在新能源汽車等領域的貿易投資合作質量和水平，實現共同發展。（商務部）

### 2026-07-28 22:32:11  #行業

【上合專家學者呼籲提升數智時代國際資訊保安】
2026上海合作組織媒體智庫峰會專家論壇27日在吉爾吉斯斯坦首都比什凱克舉行。與會專家學者表示，上合組織的成就證明，建立在相互理解和互利共贏基礎上的多邊合作，不僅有利於促進和平與安定，也有利於促進可持續發展。數智時代面臨前所未有的機遇和挑戰，各國需要攜手努力，共同塑造一個安全、有韌性、包容且有利於可持續發展的國際資訊環境。
“今日俄羅斯”國際媒體集團國際合作局局長瓦西里·普什科夫說，世界正經歷動盪變革，迫切需要智庫專家與媒體攜手合作，在加強相互理解、互信與睦鄰友好等方面發揮重要作用，為開展坦誠而有針對性的對話打造平臺。
白俄羅斯國立大學國際研究中心主任尼基塔·別連琴科說，隨著人工智慧技術快速發展，傳統資訊保安理念已難以應對新的挑戰，各國應重視資訊環境韌性和資訊自主能力，需要推動建立聯合監測預警機制，深化人工智慧領域研究與媒體、智庫交流，共同維護數智時代的資訊保安。
埃及金字塔政治和戰略研究中心副主任艾哈邁德·坎迪勒說，“上海精神”為世界提供了一條既區別於強權支配也不同於失序混亂的發展路徑，各國可以依託協商實現互利共贏。
寮國社會與經濟科學院社會經濟政策研究所所長吳沙萬·天特翁薩說，寮國致力於與上合組織夥伴密切合作，加強資訊共享，“更好地保護我們的數字環境和發展成果”。
尼泊爾中國研究中心秘書長烏彭德拉·高塔姆說，真誠協作創造的價值，遠大於各國獨自耕耘的總和。上合組織越來越務實、包容、以人為本，將進一步助力構建人類命運共同體。
新華社研究院院長劉剛說，25年來，上合組織在捍衛以聯合國為核心的國際體系和以國際法為基礎的國際秩序中發揮了重要作用。當前，單邊主義、保護主義持續衝擊全球秩序，地緣博弈加劇考驗區域協作效能，上合組織迎來提質升級的關鍵視窗期，需要加強合作，架起智庫溝通的橋樑。
與會專家學者認為，上合組織堅持求同存異、互利共贏、開放包容、公平正義、務實高效的原則，在確保地區安全穩定、推動地區繁榮、促進人文交流、維護國際公平正義、提升組織行動能力等方面取得了開創性成果，將在維護和平、促進發展、推動全球治理體系變革等方面發揮重要作用。
這次專家論壇由新華通訊社、“今日俄羅斯”國際媒體集團共同舉辦。來自中國、俄羅斯、吉爾吉斯斯坦、哈薩克、巴基斯坦、印度、斯里蘭卡、阿聯酋、寮國等十餘個國家的相關專家學者與會。（小故事）

【Recycled copper market tightens as premium scrap snapped up on arrival】Traders in Taizhou, Zhejiang report sustained copper price gains and acute demand for high‑purity scrap; premium scrap consignments are being bought the moment they arrive. Dealers are dismantling motors, cables and radiator units and sorting metal streams to meet downstream appetite. Demand expansion from EVs, AI and other industrial users, combined with constrained growth in primary copper smelting capacity, is broadening demand for recycled copper and creating opportunities in China’s scrap recovery market. ### 2026-07-28 22:16:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Richmond Fed Manufacturing Index #Importance 1

US Fifth District Manufacturing Edges Up in July — The Federal Reserve's Fifth District manufacturing index edged up to 5 in July 2026 from 4 in June, remaining well below market expectations of 9. Manufacturing activity was broadly stable during the month, as stronger shipments (8 vs. 4) and a rebound in employment (2 vs. -1) offset slower growth in new orders (5 vs. 8). Local business conditions improved sharply, with the index rising to 10 from -1. On the price front, cost pressures eased, as the growth rate of prices paid slowed to 6.08% from 6.99%, while prices received moderated to 3.96% from 4.57%. Looking ahead, firms expect inflationary pressures to moderate over the next 12 months. However, optimism softened, with expectations for local business conditions (19 vs. 22), shipments (33 vs. 36), and new orders (31 vs. 33) all declining, though remaining firmly in positive territory.

Richmond Fed Services Index Remains Flat in July — The Richmond Fed Fifth District Service Sector Survey was broadly unchanged in July 2026, marking a second consecutive month of flat activity. The revenues index slipped to -3 from 0, while the demand index edged up to 5 from 4. Current local business conditions improved slightly to -5 from -7, although expectations weakened, with the future business conditions index falling to 9 from 16. Employment growth moderated, as the current employment index declined to 4 from 8, while the forward-looking employment gauge was little changed at 23, pointing to continued hiring plans. Wage growth remained firm, with both current and expected wage indexes staying in positive territory. Price pressures eased slightly, as the growth rate of prices paid slowed, while prices received rose marginally. Firms expect both measures to moderate further over the next 12 months.

【Consumer confidence slips again — is US consumption resilience faltering?】The Conference Board's July consumer confidence index fell to 90.8, below consensus 92.3 and June's 91.2. The decline was modest and does not signal a sudden consumer collapse, but the index's prolonged low level indicates high inflation, higher rates and job-market uncertainty continue to weigh on households. In June, the current-business-and-employment assessment dropped to 116.4 and the share saying jobs are hard to find rose to the highest since January 2021; the expectations subindex recovered to 74.4 but remains below the 80 recession-warning threshold. Market takeaway: the print is mildly dollar- and yield-negative and slightly supportive of easing expectations, but the miss is limited — decisive policy implications should wait for upcoming GDP, PCE and jobs data.

TSX Slips from Record High — The S&P/TSX Composite Index edged lower on Tuesday after closing at a record high in the previous session, pressured by losses in the mining, financial, and energy sectors. Gold prices fell as a stronger US dollar and mounting uncertainty ahead of Wednesday's Federal Reserve policy decision weighed on the metal. Agnico Eagle, Barrick, and WPM each lost nearly 3%. Financials also retreated, with RBC, TD Bank, BMO, CIBC, and Brookfield down about 1%. Energy shares weakened as oil prices declined on growing hopes for a resolution to the US-Iran conflict. Imperial Oil and Cenovus fell nearly 1%, while Canadian Natural lost more than 0.5%. In contrast, technology stocks outperformed after Celestica (+1%). Shopify gained 2% and Constellation Software rose 2.5%. TFI International (+7%) also beat forecasts. Great-West Lifeco (+0.1%), First Quantum (-4%), Cardinal Energy (-1%), and Intact Financial (+1%) are due to report earnings after the closing bell.

The committee said faster zero‑day discovery is making traditional notify‑and‑patch defenses inadequate and called for stronger unknown‑threat detection, attack‑surface management, defense‑in‑depth, behavioral monitoring and rapid response, plus broader use of AI to improve vulnerability治理. It urged continued tracking of AI risks, tighter integration of technology with frontline defenses and accelerated construction of a new critical‑infrastructure security framework adapted to intelligentized attack‑defense dynamics.

### 2026-07-28 21:59:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Cotton Falls from One-Week High — Cotton prices fell below 81 cents per pound after reaching a one-week high on July 23rd, as easing weather concerns dampened expectations of tighter supplies. In India, a revival in monsoon rains accelerated cotton planting, after the having arrived three days late. Following its fifth driest June on record, the country's rainfall deficit narrowed to 16% from nearly 40% at the end of June, as widespread rains in recent weeks helped provide rain-fed irrigation to farmland's, easing fears of a supply shortfall. Meanwhile, lower oil prices weighed on demand, reducing cotton's competitiveness against polyester fibers. However, India's cotton-sown area was still down 4% year-on-year at 9.87 million hectares, reflecting slower planting across several major producing states. In Brazil, growers are expected to reduce cotton planted area by 4% in the 2025/26 season despite record exports in 2024/25, as weaker prices discouraged acreage expansion.

【我國將建設國有文物資源資料登記管理平臺】記者7月28日從國家文物局舉行的新聞釋出會上獲悉，國家文物局將按照新出臺的《國有文物資源資料管理辦法》規定，建設國有文物資源資料登記管理平臺，編制登記管理制度，統籌佈局國家及區域儲存中心，確保政策落地見效，充分釋放國有文物資源資料要素價值。近年來，社會各界對文物資料的需求日益增長。國家文物局科技教育司司長羅靜介紹，《國有文物資源資料管理辦法》明晰了國有文物資源資料權益主體，明確了國有文物資源資料責任主體，構建了資料採集、加工、儲存、傳輸、登記、服務全生命週期管理制度，對實現文物資料權益清晰、開放有序、利用有效、安全可控的科學管理目標，指導文物行業開展文物數字化實踐具有重要意義。辦法還強調國有文物管理機構應遵循公正、公平、便民的原則，面向社會提供主動公開、授權使用等服務，推動文物保護與數字化發展成果更多更公平惠及廣大群眾。（新華社）

【SpaceX shares about 20% below IPO; market value down $1.2 trillion from peak】SpaceX (SPCX.O) shares trade roughly 20% below their IPO price. On Tuesday morning the stock fell more than 5% to about $107, extending the sharp post-IPO drop from June highs. Market capitalization has declined roughly $1.2 trillion from its June 16 peak, among the largest market-cap evaporations on record. Investors have rotated away from high-risk tech amid heightened geopolitical tensions and concerns that heavy AI-related spending has distorted supply-demand and valuations in the sector. Market participants are also watching an Aug. 6 lock-up expiry that will free about 911.5 million shares valued at roughly $116 billion.

Ibovespa Jumps on Soft Mid-Month Inflation — The Ibovespa rose 1% to trade above 177,000 on Tuesday after Brazil's mid-July inflation report came in below expectations. Annual inflation slowed to 4.52%, below forecasts of 4.67% and down from 4.8% in June. The softer reading, together with June's lower-than-expected inflation data, reinforced expectations of a 25-basis-point Selic rate cut next week. Financials posted gains, with Itaú, Itaúsa, and Banco do Brasil up around 1.5%, while Bradesco gained nearly 2%. Bradesco and Santander (+2%) are due to report earnings on Wednesday. Utilities also advanced, with Axia rising nearly 3% and Sabesp adding almost 1.5%. Ambev gained about 2% ahead of its earnings release on Thursday. WEG extended recent gains, rising 1% after Goldman Sachs upgraded the stock to Neutral from Sell, citing more balanced market expectations. The company secured a contract from ENGIE Brasil to supply generating units for the R$1.2 billion Jaguara hydroelectric plant expansion project.

Brent Falls Further — Brent crude oil fell to around $86 per barrel on Tuesday, touching its lowest level in more than a week, as renewed diplomatic efforts over the Strait of Hormuz improved the outlook for Middle East oil supplies. Iran’s Foreign Minister Abbas Araghchi held separate talks with his Saudi and Omani counterparts, focusing on restoring security in the strategic waterway after disruptions caused by the US-Iran conflict. Oman has proposed a joint regional mechanism to manage the strait, allowing Iran to collect voluntary transit fees, a plan that has reportedly gained support from Gulf states. Also, President Donald Trump said Washington was holding “good talks” with Tehran, although he warned military strikes could resume if diplomacy fails, while Iran also threatened retaliation. Despite the diplomatic progress, regional risks remain elevated after Saudi Arabia intercepted drones targeting oil facilities and Yemen’s Houthi rebels claimed an attack on the kingdom’s East-West pipeline.

Chip Stocks Extend Selloff — US equity indices were mixed on Tuesday as chip producers extended their selloff, contrasting with relief on other sectors. The S&P 500 was flat, while the Nasdaq 100 dropped 1% and the Dow gained 300 points. Concerns of slowing spending on AI infrastructure and the circularity of data center investments grappled compute and memory producers, with Micron, Sandisk dropping more than 7%. The sector was also punished by optimism on Chinese competitors following the strong CXMT IPO in Shanghai. Nvidia fell 1.5% and AMD lost around 5%. On the other hand, software-facing and non-tech shares were mostly higher, with Microsoft and Meta inching higher ahead of their earnings tomorrow. Credit costs extended their pullback, tracking lower energy pries as US officials signaled willingness for diplomacy with Iran. JPMorgan was higher to set the pace for the banks, while Visa gained 1% ahead of its earning release after the close. Also, Coca Cola gained 3% after boosting its outlook.

Silver Slides on Stronger Dollar, Fed Rate Uncertainty — Silver prices fell about 2% to around $57 an ounce on Tuesday, pressured by a stronger US dollar and investor caution ahead of Wednesday's Federal Reserve policy decision. The dollar remained near a four-week high as markets priced in more than a one-third chance of a rate hike, an unusually high level of uncertainty this close to a Fed meeting despite President Donald Trump's repeated calls for lower interest rates. Investors also continue to assign roughly an 80% probability to a rate increase in September, reinforcing expectations that US borrowing costs could stay elevated. Meanwhile, optimism over a potential easing of tensions between the US and Iran reduced demand for safe-haven assets. On Monday, Trump said Washington was engaged in "good talks" with Iran and that a deal to resolve the conflict was possible, while warning that military strikes could resume if negotiations break down.

【阿曼提出聯合管理霍爾木茲海峽新機制】一名海灣地區訊息人士28日披露，阿曼向伊朗提出了一項聯合管理霍爾木茲海峽航運的地區機制，通行海峽的船隻可基於自願原則捐款。目前，阿曼和伊朗官方均未證實這一訊息。這名不願公開姓名的訊息人士表示，新機制參考馬六甲海峽的管理辦法，使用方自願向維護航道的相關基金捐款，用於環境保護和搜救工作等。阿曼的最新提議得到了一些地區國家支援。暫不清楚伊朗會如何回應這一提議。伊朗外交部長阿拉格齊27日與阿曼以及沙烏地阿拉伯外交大臣分別通話，討論霍爾木茲海峽事宜。根據伊朗外交部發表的宣告，阿拉格齊在通話中強調“加強合作的必要性”。阿曼外交代表團24日抵達伊朗首都德黑蘭。據悉，阿曼此訪旨在建立霍爾木茲海峽航運管理的適當機制。據伊朗方面26日訊息，伊朗外交部發言人巴加埃表示，伊朗與阿曼就管理霍爾木茲海峽安全航運的共同原則及操作機制進行討論，會談“富有成效並取得一些進展”。他說，阿曼代表團已於25日離開德黑蘭，但雙方技術和政治磋商仍將繼續。國際市場服務機構克普勒公司資料顯示，儘管美國和伊朗暫停相互襲擊，24日至26日，霍爾木茲海峽的每日貨船通行量均為個位數。另據伊朗知情官員訊息，27日凌晨，有6艘關閉導航和定位系統的船隻試圖經霍爾木茲海峽南部一條“非法且不安全”的航道航行，其中1艘發生事故，其餘船隻被“引導”返回波斯灣。（央視新聞）

【招商證券：韓國股市去槓桿加速，A股政策底顯現迎穩市訊號】韓國股市槓桿資金正經歷快速收縮。韓國金融投資協會資料顯示，全市場信用融資餘額自6月24日的38.6萬億韓元歷史峰值，至7月24日已回落至32.67萬億韓元，降幅達15.4%。其中，KOSDAQ板塊收縮更為顯著（降幅22%），快於KOSPI主機板（-13.5%）。在科技股暴跌引發的強制平倉潮中，自6月19日以來累計強平金額達1.1萬億韓元。值得注意的是，槓桿ETF的收縮速度遠超融資餘額，成為市場下跌的高效“加速器”。以SAMSUNG KODEX 2倍做多ETF為例，其規模從6月高點驟降48%至38億元，其機械式的每日再平衡機制形成了無間斷的自我強化迴路。外部槓桿去化雖對A股情緒形成一定傳導，但國內穩市訊號正密集釋放，A股政策底已然顯現。一方面，證監會堅定維護資本市場平穩健康執行；另一方面，北京國管、北京控股等多家央國企及上市公司集中披露增持、回購及提高分紅安排，彰顯發展信心。資金面上，上週寬基ETF大幅淨流入超500億元。往後看，隨著穩市座談會召開，監管有望進一步加大穩市力度。若後續市場超預期下行，穩市措施有望對標去年4月，從資金支援、央企回購、金融機構增持等多方面入手，以穩定資本市場並增強投資者信心。

【甘肅局地啟動洪水防禦Ⅲ級應急響應】記者從甘肅省水利廳獲悉，受降雨和上游來水影響，甘肅省酒泉市肅北蒙古族自治縣境內黨城灣鎮段水位快速上漲，黨河黨城灣水文站7月28日16時流量311立方米每秒，超過洪水橙色預警標準250立方米每秒，接近紅色預警標準350立方米每秒。根據《甘肅省水利廳水旱災害防禦應急預案》規定，甘肅省水利廳決定7月28日17時起，針對酒泉市啟動洪水防禦Ⅲ級應急響應。 甘肅省水利廳、甘肅省氣象局7月28日13時釋出山洪災害氣象風險藍色預警，預計28日13時至29日14時，酒泉市（瓜州縣、肅北縣、阿克塞縣、玉門市、敦煌市）區域性地方降水量可達大到暴雨，可能發生山洪災害。其他地區也可能因局地短歷時強降水引發山洪災害。 甘肅省水利廳提示，相關部門要注意做好實時監測、防汛預警，緊盯河流、水庫水位，加密水情監測，做好涉河涉溝在建工程和沿河作業場所洪水防禦工作，確保人民群眾生命財產安全。（新華社）

### 2026-07-28 21:23:51  #其他

【貸款利率定價有了新“尺子” 全國首單已落地】近日，三家銀行在海南落地了全國首單三筆DR（存款類金融機構間債券回購利率）定價貸款。具體來看，工商銀行海南省分行、招商銀行海口分行、浦發銀行海口分行三家銀行立足海南自貿港跨境經營主體集聚、EF賬戶體系較為成熟、金融開放場景豐富等優勢，主動探索DR基準利率貸款創新應用，在海南自貿港落地全國首單三筆DR基準利率貸款。當前，我國銀行的貸款利率主要是在LPR基礎上加點而來。而本次三家銀行的嘗試被視為一種新的可能性，為深化利率市場化改革提供了可複製、可推廣的海南樣本。“DR屬於交易形成型利率，區別於LPR的報價型利率。”一位業內人士向記者解釋。DR依託海量市場交易生成，單一主體難以干預，市場化程度更高，這也意味著貸款利率定價機制從報價型向交易型轉變。（金融時報）

【藍龍蝦降價近20%】藍龍蝦，原產澳大利亞，國內部分地區已有大規模養殖。廣東珠三角區域是國內藍龍蝦主產區之一，眼下正值消費旺季。在廣東佛山高明區的一處蝦塘，養殖戶告訴央視財經記者，每天都有幾個貿易商過來拿蝦，目前38元到40元一斤。從廣州趕來採購的貿易商告訴記者，今年市場行情向好，隨著養殖成本降低，藍龍蝦的價格下行帶動銷量明顯上漲。各種規格藍龍蝦的價格普遍下降20%左右。記者走訪發現，隨著育苗技術實現突破，蝦苗成本較往年明顯下降，藍龍蝦養殖經濟效益持續提升，不少養殖戶紛紛擴大規模。資料顯示，2024年廣東佛山高明區養殖藍龍蝦達2100多畝，2026年躍升至6000畝，預計今年產值接近2億元。（央視財經）

### 2026-07-28 21:17:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Case Shiller Home Price Index YoY #Importance 2

US Home Price Growth Accelerates in May — The S&P Cotality Case-Shiller 20-City Home Price Index rose 1.6% year-over-year in May 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 1.2% increase in April and exceeding market expectations of 1.3%. The annual gain was the strongest since August 2025. However, after adjusting for inflation, home prices declined for a 12th consecutive month, as May's 4.2% inflation rate outpaced nominal home price growth by 2.6 percentage points. For a third straight month, Chicago recorded the strongest annual price increase among the 20 cities, up 6.9%, followed by New York (4.2%) and Cleveland (3.1%). Meanwhile, Las Vegas posted the steepest decline (-1.9%), ahead of Seattle (-1.8%), Denver (-1.8%), and Tampa (-1.6%). On a monthly basis, home prices increased 0.9%, easing slightly from gains of 1.0% in April and 1.1% in March but continuing to reflect the seasonal strength typically seen during the spring homebuying season. On a seasonally adjusted basis, prices rose 0.2% from the previous month.

### 2026-07-28 21:10:03  #其他

【世界首臺630攝氏度超超臨界煤電機組投運】中國大唐28日宣佈，大唐鄆城630攝氏度超超臨界二次再熱煤電機組當日全容量投運，這是世界首個再熱蒸汽溫度超過630攝氏度、發電熱效率突破50%的超超臨界煤電專案，單臺機組容量100萬千瓦。 中國大唐董事長呂軍表示，這一機組創造了同型別機組溫度最高、壓力最高、效率最高、供電煤耗最低的世界紀錄，進一步提高燃煤效率，降低度電耗煤量。 呂軍介紹，機組以技術革新、工藝升級、裝備迭代、材料創新為抓手，實現“新技術、新工藝、新材料、新裝備”突破與整合應用，裝置國產化率達98.89%，成功突破發電熱效率50%的行業技術“天花板”，在效率、溫度、壓力、煤耗等方面實現新突破。 這一專案是中國大唐聯合東方電氣、中國寶武、中國鋼研等全產業鏈聯合攻關取得的重大技術突破。採用多種新型耐高溫特種鋼材和自主研製C630R新型汽輪機轉子，同步整合應用變速小機帶功率平衡發電機雙機回熱系統、12級回熱抽汽系統等核心技術。 專案位於山東省鄆城縣，是“十三五”期間的國家重點專案，安裝2臺100萬千瓦超超臨界二次再熱燃煤發電機組，是我國攻克煤電關鍵核心技術的標誌性成果，為構建新型電力系統提供有力支撐。(新華社)

### 2026-07-28 21:08:34  #宏觀 #國際

【王毅出席全球發展倡議5週年高階別會議】
2026年7月28日，中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅在北京出席全球發展倡議5週年高階別會議並致辭。

王毅表示，5年前，面對全球發展事業何去何從的時代之問，習近平主席鄭重提出全球發展倡議，為重振全球發展事業提供了中國方案、作出了中國貢獻。5年來，倡議的理念引領效應持續釋放，各領域合作機制不斷完善，務實合作成果接連落地。當前國際形勢變亂交織，全球發展倡議的時代意義更加凸顯。我們要繼續高舉發展旗幟，堅定推進全球發展倡議與聯合國發展議程對接，共同創造美好未來。
一是加強團結合作，營造和平發展環境。中方將一如既往支援聯合國改革振興，強化發展支柱，增進廣大發展中國家福祉。

二是凝聚行動合力，加快落實2030年議程。中方將繼續向其他發展中國家提供更多的發展資源，推動解決發展不平衡不充分問題。

三是推進開放互鑑，壯大創新發展引擎。中方願同各方深化創新合作，助力全球南方充分把握新機遇、妥善應對新挑戰。

四是踐行多邊主義，完善全球治理體系。要加強政策協調，確保聯合國2030年後全球發展議程更好反映全球南方的普遍需求。

王毅強調，中國的發展離不開世界，世界的發展也離不開中國。無論國際形勢如何變化，中方將繼續發揮負責任大國作用，將自身發展同各國共同發展緊密聯絡起來，做全球發展的促進者和貢獻者，不斷推進落實全球發展倡議，同世界各國共享發展機遇，推動構建人類命運共同體。

本次會議由外交部、國家國際發展合作署、聯合國發展系統駐華機構聯合舉辦，主題為“全球發展倡議5週年再出發，構建可持續發展的美好未來”。聯合國副秘書長、秘書長辦公廳主任拉特雷，“全球發展倡議之友小組”成員國駐華使節，聯合國發展機構駐華代表等出席。各方高度評價5年來全球發展倡議取得的豐碩成果，讚賞中國在推進國際發展合作中發揮的引領作用，認為倡議完全契合聯合國憲章宗旨，有助於推動將發展置於全球議程的中心位置，為全球南方國家提供了寶貴機遇，願同中方持續深化發展合作，共同踐行多邊主義，攜手促進世界和平與繁榮。（外交部）

### 2026-07-28 20:44:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Goods Trade Balance #Importance 2

US Goods Trade Deficit Narrows in June — The US goods trade deficit narrowed to $101.5 billion in June 2026 from a 14-month high of $105.9 billion in May, according to preliminary data. Exports fell 1.8% to $204.7 billion, marking a second straight monthly decline and moving further below April's record high, driven by lower shipments of industrial supplies (-4.4%), foods, feeds, and beverages (-3.1%), and capital goods (-1.1%). Imports declined at a faster 2.6% to $306.2 billion, with purchases falling across all major categories, including consumer goods (-3.8%), capital goods (-2.0%), industrial supplies (-1.9%), vehicles (-2.5%), and foods, feeds, and beverages (-2.5%). In the first half of 2026, the cumulative goods trade deficit narrowed to $535.5 billion from $716.6 billion in the same period a year earlier. The improvement suggests trade flows are continuing to normalize following last year's US tariff announcements, although uncertainty surrounding US trade policy remains.

### 2026-07-28 20:42:32  #MKT News #Star Stocks #Apple #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #AAPL.O #Impact mixed

【Apple shifts from device purchase to monthly leasing — will it shorten upgrade cycles?】Apple has rolled out Apple Upgrade in the US, offering device leases via Klarna. Terms: iPhone and Apple Watch 12–24 months; Mac and iPad 24–36 months, matching typical replacement/use cycles. Leasing lowers the monthly payment barrier and embeds return, renewal or upgrade options at term, which could convert irregular upgrade demand into a more predictable update cadence. Lower monthly fees do not necessarily reduce total ownership cost; long-term holders may be no better off than with interest-free instalments. Whether the plan shortens upgrade cycles depends on adoption, end‑of‑term upgrade conversion and sustained renewal rates. Only repeated re‑enrolment would let Apple shift hardware from one‑off sales toward a steadier replacement-driven revenue pattern.

### 2026-07-28 20:41:14  #國際

【多瑙河布達佩斯段水位創歷史新低】匈牙利水資源管理總局28日釋出的資料顯示，多瑙河布達佩斯段水位已降至31釐米，創下該河段水位歷史新低。由於水位下降，多瑙河沿線多地相繼發現了二戰時期的彈藥和沉船等。 水利部門當天測得的資料打破了該河段2018年10月25日創下的33釐米最低紀錄。氣象與水利專家預測，未來幾天，多瑙河水位可能繼續下降。 鑑於罕見的高溫乾旱重新整理了水位紀錄，近日在多瑙河上的瑪格麗特島附近水域清理出一些二戰時期遺留炮彈。此外，據當地媒體報道，多瑙河沿線多地近期相繼發現了一些沉船殘骸。 近來持續高溫與降水不足使歐洲主要河流水位下降。國際科研機構“世界天氣歸因聯盟”23日釋出研究報告說，人類活動導致的氣候變化加劇了歐洲當前的嚴重乾旱。與降雨量不足相比，異常高溫是歐洲多地乾旱加重的更重要原因。(新華社)

US Wholesale Inventories Rise More than Expected — US wholesale inventories rose 0.3% month-over-month to $945.9 billion in June 2026, matching an upwardly revised 0.3% increase in May and exceeding market expectations of a 0.2% gain. According to preliminary data, inventories increased for a fifth consecutive month, supported by a 0.7% rise in durable goods stocks after a 0.2% increase in the previous month. Meanwhile, nondurable goods inventories fell 0.4%, reversing May's 0.4% gain. On an annual basis, wholesale inventories were up 4.4%.

【河南“三支一扶”高分成績引爭議，官方再回應】據“河南人社”，河南省“三支一扶”領導小組協調辦公室7月28日再發宣告：近期，部分省轄市在公佈2026年省“三支一扶”人員招募面試資格確認人員名單時，有的省轄市包含筆試成績，有的省轄市僅公佈了人員名單、無筆試成績。為切實增強考試公開透明度，切實維護考試的公平公正，經河南省“三支一扶”工作領導小組協調辦公室研究決定，凡未公佈筆試成績的省轄市，統一於2026年7月30日15點之前，由各省轄市將進入面試資格確認人員名單及其筆試成績一併對外公佈。同時，針對網民質疑的考試嫌疑，省“三支一扶”領導小組協調辦公室正會同有關部門進行深入核查，查實一個取消一個，並依規依法處理。崗位空缺時，依考生報考崗位成績由高到低依次遞補進入面試資格人員。7月27日，河南省“三支一扶”工作領導小組協調辦公室釋出宣告：近日，網路平臺出現我省安陽、平頂山、駐馬店、濟源等地2026年高校畢業生“三支一扶”高分考生集中等相關輿情資訊，引發廣大考生、家長及社會各界關注。我們對此高度重視，第一時間成立工作組，針對網傳內容開展全面、嚴謹的調查核實工作，儘快查明真相，及時通報情況，如發現違規違紀問題，將移交有關部門嚴肅查處，切實保障廣大考生合法權益、維護考試公平公正。此前，據荔枝新聞報道，近日，河南“三支一扶”考試成績公佈，有網友在安陽市和濟源市的考生成績中發現規律，多個崗位出現高分斷層領先，第一名80多分，而第二名只有60多分。該網友指出“一人崗就有一個人考80分以上，兩人崗就有兩個人考80分以上”。

### 2026-07-28 20:21:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Mid-month Inflation Rate MoM #Importance 1

Brazil Mid-Month Inflation Comes in Below Forecasts — Consumer prices in Brazil rose 0.06% in the month to mid-July 2026, slowing sharply from 0.41% in June and coming in below forecasts of 0.2%. The reading marked the lowest mid-month inflation rate since August 2025. Food and beverage prices provided the largest downward contribution, falling 0.66%. Food consumed at home dropped 1.14%, while food away from home accelerated to 0.55%. Housing was the main source of inflationary pressure, rising 0.97%, driven by a 3.03% increase in residential electricity prices following the introduction of the yellow tariff flag and regulated tariff adjustments in several cities. Transportation prices rose 0.11%, as an 11.7% jump in airfares more than offset lower fuel prices amid easing concerns over the Middle East oil supply crisis. Health and personal care costs increased 0.28%, led by higher personal hygiene product prices. The 12-month inflation rate eased to 4.52% from 4.58% in June.

【US media: Netanyahu's influence on Trump much reduced during this US visit】Axios reports Israeli Prime Minister Netanyahu will meet Trump in the Oval Office on Tuesday — their eighth meeting in Trump’s second term — but says Netanyahu’s sway over Trump is markedly weaker than on prior visits. The meeting could still matter as Trump weighs next steps, including possible coordinated action between the two countries. During the opening months after the Iran war began the men spoke almost daily to coordinate; Axios says call frequency has fallen sharply in recent weeks and Netanyahu now leans more on Secretary of State Rubio to read Trump’s thinking. Netanyahu reportedly struggled to secure the Tuesday slot after failing to visit last week. Since the April 8 ceasefire, Axios says Netanyahu’s influence on Trump’s Iran war policy has waned; Trump on Monday acknowledged differences with Netanyahu, and two weeks ago Netanyahu failed to convince Trump to drop an F-35 sale to Turkey.

### 2026-07-28 20:14:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macedonia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

North Macedonia Holds Key Policy Rate at 4.25% — The National Bank of North Macedonia left its interest rate unchanged at 4.25% at its July 2026 meeting, citing uncertainty surrounding foreign prices and geopolitical tensions. Although headline inflation slowed in June to 3.4% from 4.8%, driven by lower food prices, policymakers stated more time was needing to assess price stability considering that inflation remains elevated, as foreign prices are still high and unstable due to geopolitical tensions and possible spill-over effects. Economic activity continues to show resilience, although growth slowed to 3.1% in the first quarter of 2026 from 3.8% in the prior quarter. Looking ahead, the NBNM stated it will continue to aim for stability of the denar against the euro, in order to ensur price stability in the medium term, and will closely monitor domestic and international economic and financial development.

### 2026-07-28 20:02:03  #MKT News

【APEC forestry ministers adopt joint statement】The 2026 APEC forestry ministers meeting, held in Shenzhen on 27–28, adopted a joint statement calling for enhanced biodiversity protection and stronger enforcement against illegal trade in forest products. It urges economies to embed forest governance and sustainable forest management into economic development plans and policy frameworks. The statement asks members to expand finance and investment opportunities for forestry development and legal timber trade, tailored to national circumstances, and to support upgrading processing and value chains for forest products. It promotes deeper Asia‑Pacific cooperation on urban forestry and water‑resource management, and calls for using technological innovation and sustainable management to restore ecosystems and reduce habitat fragmentation. Under principles of sustainable forest management, it encourages development of emerging forest industries, products and services.
